	desc = cosmo_prostitutes_events.11.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_prostitutes_events.11.a
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = europe_prostitute_1a
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = prostitute_available_tt 
		}

			limit = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = europe_prostitute_1a
		}

	desc = cosmo_prostitutes_events.34.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_EAST_INDIES_MENU_4_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_prostitutes_events.34.a
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = east_indies_prostitute_4a
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = prostitute_available_tt 
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = east_indies_prostitute_4a
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = prostitute_sent_province_tt 		
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_modifier = east_indies_prostitute_4a
				}
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = cosmo_prostitute_check
					duration = 90  
					desc = cosmo_prostitute_check_DESC
					hidden = no	
				}
			}
		}						
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_prostitutes_events.34.b
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = east_indies_prostitute_4b

	hidden = yes

		ai = no
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_prostitutes_events.40.a

			limit = {
		OR = {

			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_1
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_prostitutes_events.41
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_1
		}		
		has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_2
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_prostitutes_events.42
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_1
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_2
		}		
		has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_3
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_prostitutes_events.43
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_all
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_1
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_2
			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_3
		}		
		has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_4
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_prostitutes_events.44

		}
		if = {

			has_country_flag = brothel_indochina_menu_2
